Translation - Chapter 214

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a translation algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 773. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 989/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 773 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the translation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 989. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a translation algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 272. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 493/3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the translation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^272 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 493-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
